Choice text -> NextScene\n2. [Tag] Choice text with tag -> AnotherScene\n```\nCreates a \"choice\" node with multiple options:\n- Each option is numbered (1, 2, 3, etc.)\n- Options can have `[Tags]` like `[Lie]`, `[Attack]` for special actions\n- Each choice transitions to its target scene\n\n## Implementation Pattern\n\n### Step 1: Parse Scene Blocks\n```python\nimport re\n\ndef parse_scenes(text: str) -> dict:\n    # Split by scene headers [SceneName]\n    scene_pattern = r'\\[([^\\]]+)\\]'\n    scenes = {}\n\n    # Find all scene headers and their positions\n    for match in re.finditer(scene_pattern, text):\n        scene_name = match.group(1)\n        start_pos = match.end()\n        scenes[scene_name] = start_pos\n\n    return scenes\n```\n\n### Step 2: Extract Scene Content\n```python\ndef get_scene_content(text: str, scene_name: str, all_scenes: dict) -> str:\n    start = all_scenes[scene_name]\n    # Find next scene start\n    current_idx = list(all_scenes.keys()).index(scene_name)\n\n    if current_idx < len(all_scenes) - 1:\n        next_scene_name = list(all_scenes.keys())[current_idx + 1]\n        end = all_scenes[next_scene_name] - len(f\"[{next_scene_name}]\")\n    else:\n        end = len(text)\n\n    return text[start:end].strip()\n```\n\n### Step 3: Parse Lines and Choices\n```python\ndef parse_scene_lines(content: str, scene_name: str):\n    lines = []\n\n    # Pattern for speaker lines: \"Speaker: text -> Target\"\n    speaker_pattern = r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    # Pattern for choice lines: \"1. text -> Target\"\n    choice_pattern = r'^\\d+\\.\\s*(\\[.+?\\]\\s*)?(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    for line in content.split('\\n'):\n        line = line.strip()\n        if not line:\n            continue\n\n        # Try to match speaker line\n        match = re.match(speaker_pattern, line)\n        if match:\n            speaker, text, target = match.groups()\n            lines.append({\n                'type': 'line',\n                'speaker': speaker.strip(),\n                'text': text.strip(),\n                'target': target.strip()\n            })\n\n        # Try to match choice line\n        match = re.match(choice_pattern, line)\n        if match:\n            tag, text, target = match.groups()\n            choice_text = f\"{tag.strip() if tag else ''} {text.strip()}\".strip()\n            lines.append({\n                'type': 'choice',\n                'text': choice_text,\n                'target': target.strip()\n            })\n\n    return lines\n```\n\n## Key Regex Patterns\n\n| Pattern | Purpose | Example |\n|---------|---------|---------|\n| `\\[([^\\]]+)\\]` | Scene headers | `[SceneName]` |\n| `^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$` | Speaker lines | `Guard: Halt! -> NextScene` |\n| `^\\d+\\.\\s*(\\[.+?\\]\\s*)?(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$` | Choice lines | `1. [Lie] I'm a merchant -> Target` |\n\n## Common Issues and Solutions\n\n### Issue: Handling multiline dialogue\n**Solution**: Combine consecutive lines without transitions until you hit a `->` arrow.\n\n### Issue: Preserving tags like [Lie] and [Attack]\n**Solution**: Keep tags as part of the choice text or in a separate field.\n\n### Issue: Scene name spacing\n**Solution**: Always strip whitespace from parsed scene names and targets.\n\n## Testing\n\n```python\ntest_script = \"\"\"\n[Start]\nGuard: Hello -> Choice1\n\n[Choice1]\n1. Yes -> End\n2. No -> Start\n\"\"\"\n\nscenes = parse_scenes(test_script)\nassert 'Start' in scenes\nassert 'Choice1' in scenes\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":299,"cache_read_input_tokens":19015,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":299,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":1},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"070817b2-f40c-4c5b-aefa-393b6d2712fb","uuid":"3bcaeead-7d4b-44df-b02b-3c48a851bd77"}

Unique identifier from [SceneName]\n    \"text\": \"dialogue text\",      # The actual text content\n    \"speaker\": \"Character\",      # Speaker name (empty for choices)\n    \"type\": \"line\" or \"choice\"  # Node type\n}\n```\n\n### Edge Format\n```python\n{\n    \"from\": \"source_scene_id\",\n    \"to\": \"target_scene_id\",\n    \"text\": \"transition_text\"  # Optional: label text\n}\n```\n\n### Graph Output Format\n```python\n{\n    \"nodes\": [\n        {\"id\": \"...\", \"text\": \"...\", \"speaker\": \"...\", \"type\": \"line\"},\n        {\"id\": \"...\", \"text\": \"...\", \"speaker\": \"\", \"type\": \"choice\"}\n    ],\n    \"edges\": [\n        {\"from\": \"...\", \"to\": \"...\", \"text\": \"\"}\n    ]\n}\n```\n\n## Building the Graph\n\n### Step 1: Create Nodes from Scenes\n```python\ndef create_nodes(parsed_scenes: dict) -> list:\n    nodes = []\n    node_id = 0\n\n    for scene_name, lines in parsed_scenes.items():\n        for line in lines:\n            node = {\n                \"id\": f\"{node_id}\",\n                \"text\": line.get('text', ''),\n                \"speaker\": line.get('speaker', ''),\n                \"type\": line.get('type', 'line')\n            }\n            nodes.append(node)\n            line['node_id'] = str(node_id)  # Store for edge creation\n            node_id += 1\n\n    return nodes\n```\n\n### Step 2: Create Edges from Transitions\n```python\ndef create_edges(parsed_scenes: dict, nodes: list) -> list:\n    edges = []\n    node_map = {node['id']: node for node in nodes}\n\n    for scene_name, lines in parsed_scenes.items():\n        for i, line in enumerate(lines):\n            if 'target' in line and line['target']:\n                source_id = line.get('node_id')\n                target_id = None\n\n                # Find target node ID\n                for node in nodes:\n                    if node's scene is line['target']:\n                        target_id = node['id']\n                        break\n\n                if source_id and target_id:\n                    edge = {\n                        \"from\": source_id,\n                        \"to\": target_id,\n                        \"text\": \"\"\n                    }\n                    edges.append(edge)\n\n    return edges\n```\n\n### Step 3: Merge Adjacent Lines in Same Scene\nWhen a scene contains multiple speaker lines without branching, they can be merged into one node:\n\n```python\ndef merge_consecutive_lines(parsed_scenes: dict) -> dict:\n    merged = {}\n\n    for scene_name, lines in parsed_scenes.items():\n        merged_lines = []\n        current_node = None\n\n        for line in lines:\n            if line['type'] == 'line' and current_node and current_node['type'] == 'line':\n                # Merge with previous line\n                current_node['text'] += '\\n' + line['text']\n            else:\n                if current_node:\n                    merged_lines.append(current_node)\n                current_node = line.copy()\n\n        if current_node:\n            merged_lines.append(current_node)\n\n        merged[scene_name] = merged_lines\n\n    return merged\n```\n\n## Graph Validation\n\n### Constraint 1: Reachability\nAll nodes must be reachable from the first node (DFS/BFS):\n\n```python\ndef validate_reachability(nodes: list, edges: list) -> bool:\n    if not nodes:\n        return True\n\n    first_node_id = nodes[0]['id']\n    visited = set()\n    stack = [first_node_id]\n\n    # Build adjacency list\n    adj = {node['id']: [] for node in nodes}\n    for edge in edges:\n        adj[edge['from']].append(edge['to'])\n\n    while stack:\n        node_id = stack.pop()\n        if node_id in visited:\n            continue\n        visited.add(node_id)\n        stack.extend(adj.get(node_id, []))\n\n    unreachable = set(node['id'] for node in nodes) - visited\n    if unreachable:\n        print(f\"Unreachable nodes: {unreachable}\")\n        return False\n\n    return True\n```\n\n### Constraint 2: All Edges Point to Existing Nodes\n```python\ndef validate_edges(nodes: list, edges: list) -> bool:\n    node_ids = {node['id'] for node in nodes}\n\n    for edge in edges:\n        if edge['to'] not in node_ids:\n            print(f\"Edge points to non-existent node: {edge['to']}\")\n            return False\n\n    return True\n```\n\n### Constraint 3: Multiple Paths Can Lead to \"End\"\nThis is naturally allowed in the graph structure. Verify by checking all nodes named \"End\" are reachable via multiple paths:\n\n```python\ndef has_multiple_paths_to_end(edges: list, nodes: list) -> bool:\n    # Find all \"End\" nodes\n    end_nodes = {node['id'] for node in nodes if node['id'] == 'End'}\n\n    for end_id in end_nodes:\n        # Count incoming edges\n        incoming = sum(1 for edge in edges if edge['to'] == end_id)\n        if incoming < 2:\n            print(f\"End node {end_id} has only {incoming} incoming edge(s)\")\n\n    return True  # This is more of an observation than validation\n```\n\n## Implementation Checklist\n\n- [ ] Parse all scenes and lines from text\n- [ ] Create unique node IDs for each line/choice\n- [ ] Create edges for all transitions\n- [ ] Validate all nodes are reachable from start\n- [ ] Validate all edge targets exist in nodes\n- [ ] Generate JSON output with proper formatting\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":4,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1395,"cache_read_input_tokens":19314,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1395,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"070817b2-f40c-4c5b-aefa-393b6d2712fb","uuid":"65eda7cd-f213-4936-b90f-4906050f22c4"}

Unique identifier from [SceneName]\n    \"text\": \"dialogue text\",      # The actual text content\n    \"speaker\": \"Character\",      # Speaker name (empty for choices)\n    \"type\": \"line\" or \"choice\"  # Find target node ID\n                for node in nodes:\n                    if node's scene is line['target']:\n                        target_id = node['id']\n                        break\n\n                if source_id and target_id:\n                    edge = {\n                        \"from\": source_id,\n                        \"to\": target_id,\n                        \"text\": \"\"\n                    }\n                    edges.append(edge)\n\n    return edges\n```\n\n### Step 3: Merge Adjacent Lines in Same Scene\nWhen a scene contains multiple speaker lines without branching, they can be merged into one node:\n\n```python\ndef merge_consecutive_lines(parsed_scenes: dict) -> dict:\n    merged = {}\n\n    for scene_name, lines in parsed_scenes.items():\n        merged_lines = []\n        current_node = None\n\n        for line in lines:\n            if line['type'] == 'line' and current_node and current_node['type'] == 'line':\n                # Merge with previous line\n                current_node['text'] += '\\n' + line['text']\n            else:\n                if current_node:\n                    merged_lines.append(current_node)\n                current_node = line.copy()\n\n        if current_node:\n            merged_lines.append(current_node)\n\n        merged[scene_name] = merged_lines\n\n    return merged\n```\n\n## Graph Validation\n\n### Constraint 1: Reachability\nAll nodes must be reachable from the first node (DFS/BFS):\n\n```python\ndef validate_reachability(nodes: list, edges: list) -> bool:\n    if not nodes:\n        return True\n\n    first_node_id = nodes[0]['id']\n    visited = set()\n    stack = [first_node_id]\n\n    # Build adjacency list\n    adj = {node['id']: [] for node in nodes}\n    for edge in edges:\n        adj[edge['from']].append(edge['to'])\n\n    while stack:\n        node_id = stack.pop()\n        if node_id in visited:\n            continue\n        visited.add(node_id)\n        stack.extend(adj.get(node_id, []))\n\n    unreachable = set(node['id'] for node in nodes) - visited\n    if unreachable:\n        print(f\"Unreachable nodes: {unreachable}\")\n        return False\n\n    return True\n```\n\n### Constraint 2: All Edges Point to Existing Nodes\n```python\ndef validate_edges(nodes: list, edges: list) -> bool:\n    node_ids = {node['id'] for node in nodes}\n\n    for edge in edges:\n        if edge['to'] not in node_ids:\n            print(f\"Edge points to non-existent node: {edge['to']}\")\n            return False\n\n    return True\n```\n\n### Constraint 3: Multiple Paths Can Lead to \"End\"\nThis is naturally allowed in the graph structure. Verify by checking all nodes named \"End\" are reachable via multiple paths:\n\n```python\ndef has_multiple_paths_to_end(edges: list, nodes: list) -> bool:\n    # Find all \"End\" nodes\n    end_nodes = {node['id'] for node in nodes if node['id'] == 'End'}\n\n    for end_id in end_nodes:\n        # Count incoming edges\n        incoming = sum(1 for edge in edges if edge['to'] == end_id)\n        if incoming < 2:\n            print(f\"End node {end_id} has only {incoming} incoming edge(s)\")\n\n    return True  # This is more of an observation than validation\n```\n\n## Implementation Checklist\n\n- [ ] Parse all scenes and lines from text\n- [ ] Create unique node IDs for each line/choice\n- [ ] Create edges for all transitions\n- [ ] Validate all nodes are reachable from start\n- [ ] Validate all edge targets exist in nodes\n- [ ] Generate JSON output with proper formatting\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Identify the first node\n    first_node_id = nodes[0]['id']\n\n    # Build adjacency list from edges\n    adj = {node['id']: [] for node in nodes}\n    for edge in edges:\n        adj[edge['from']].append(edge['to'])\n\n    # BFS from first node\n    visited = set()\n    queue = [first_node_id]\n\n    while queue:\n        node_id = queue.pop(0)\n        if node_id in visited:\n            continue\n\n        visited.add(node_id)\n        queue.extend(adj.get(node_id, []))\n\n    # BFS with path tracking\n    queue = [(start_id, [start_id])]\n    visited = {start_id}\n\n    while queue:\n        node_id, path = queue.pop(0)\n\n        if node_id == target_id:\n            return path\n\n        for neighbor in adj.get(node_id, []):\n            if neighbor not in visited:\n                visited.add(neighbor)\n                queue.append((neighbor, path + [neighbor]))\n\n    return None\n```\n\n## Constraint 2: All Edge Targets Exist\n\n### Definition\nEvery edge must point to a node that exists in the node list (except possibly \"End\" nodes in some formats).\n\n### Implementation\n```python\ndef validate_edge_targets(nodes: list, edges: list) -> tuple[bool, list]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Verify all edge targets exist as nodes.\n    Returns: (is_valid, missing_node_ids)\n    \"\"\"\n    node_ids = {node['id'] for node in nodes}\n    missing = []\n\n    for edge in edges:\n        # Limit for performance\n            return\n\n        if current == target:\n            paths.append(path[:])\n            return\n\n        if current in visited:\n            return\n\n        visited.add(current)\n\n        for neighbor in adj.get(current, []):\n            dfs(neighbor, target, path + [neighbor], visited.copy())\n\n    dfs(start_id, target_id, [start_id], set())\n    return paths\n```\n\n### Counting Multiple Paths\n```python\ndef count_paths_to_end(nodes: list, edges: list) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Count number of paths to each 'End' node.\"\"\"\n    end_nodes = {node['id'] for node in nodes if node['id'] == 'End'}\n\n    if not nodes:\n        return {}\n\n    first_node_id = nodes[0]['id']\n    path_counts = {}\n\n    for end_id in end_nodes:\n        paths = find_paths_to_node(first_node_id, end_id, edges)\n        path_counts[end_id] = len(paths)\n        print(f\"Node {end_id}: {len(paths)} paths found\")\n\n    return path_counts\n```\n\n## Additional Validation Checks\n\n### Check for Cycles\nDetect if the graph contains cycles (useful for story validation):\n\n```python\ndef has_cycle(edges: list) -> tuple[bool, list]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Detect if graph has cycles using DFS.\n    Returns: (has_cycle, cycle_path_if_found)\n    \"\"\"\n    adj = {}\n    for edge in edges:\n        if edge['from'] not in adj:\n            adj[edge['from']] = []\n        adj[edge['from']].append(edge['to'])\n\n    visited = set()\n    rec_stack = set()\n    cycle_path = []\n\n    def dfs(node: str, path: list) -> bool:\n        visited.add(node)\n        rec_stack.add(node)\n        path.append(node)\n\n        for neighbor in adj.get(node, []):\n            if neighbor not in visited:\n                if dfs(neighbor, path[:]):\n                    return True\n            elif neighbor in rec_stack:\n                # Found cycle\n                cycle_idx = path.index(neighbor)\n                cycle_path.extend(path[cycle_idx:] + [neighbor])\n                return True\n\n        rec_stack.discard(node)\n        return False\n\n    for node in adj:\n        if node not in visited:\n            if dfs(node, []):\n                return True, cycle_path\n\n    return False, []\n```\n\n### Check Node Connectivity\n```python\ndef get_graph_statistics(nodes: list, edges: list) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Get statistics about graph connectivity.\"\"\"\n    stats = {\n        \"total_nodes\": len(nodes),\n        \"total_edges\": len(edges),\n        \"node_types\": {},\n        \"avg_edges_per_node\": 0,\n        \"isolated_nodes\": []\n    }\n\n    # Count node types\n    for node in nodes:\n        node_type = node.get('type', 'unknown')\n        stats[\"node_types\"][node_type] = stats[\"node_types\"].get(node_type, 0) + 1\n\n    # Count edges per node\n    out_degree = {node['id']: 0 for node in nodes}\n    in_degree = {node['id']: 0 for node in nodes}\n\n    for edge in edges:\n        out_degree[edge['from']] += 1\n        in_degree[edge['to']] += 1\n\n    # Find isolated nodes (no incoming or outgoing edges except start)\n    first_node_id = nodes[0]['id'] if nodes else None\n    for node in nodes:\n        node_id = node['id']\n        if node_id != first_node_id and in_degree.get(node_id, 0) == 0:\n            stats[\"isolated_nodes\"].append(node_id)\n\n    if edges:\n        stats[\"avg_edges_per_node\"] = len(edges) / len(nodes)\n\n    return stats\n```\n\n## Comprehensive Validation\n\n```python\ndef validate_graph(nodes: list, edges: list) -> tuple[bool, dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Run all validations and return results.\n    Returns: (is_valid, validation_report)\n    \"\"\"\n    report = {\n        \"reachable\": False,\n        \"edges_valid\": False,\n        \"no_cycles\": False,\n        \"errors\": []\n    }\n\n    # Found cycle\n                cycle_idx = path.index(neighbor)\n                cycle_path.extend(path[cycle_idx:] + [neighbor])\n                return True\n\n        rec_stack.discard(node)\n        return False\n\n    for node in adj:\n        if node not in visited:\n            if dfs(node, []):\n                return True, cycle_path\n\n    return False, []\n```\n\n### Check Node Connectivity\n```python\ndef get_graph_statistics(nodes: list, edges: list) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"Get statistics about graph connectivity.\"\"\"\n    stats = {\n        \"total_nodes\": len(nodes),\n        \"total_edges\": len(edges),\n        \"node_types\": {},\n        \"avg_edges_per_node\": 0,\n        \"isolated_nodes\": []\n    }\n\n    # Count node types\n    for node in nodes:\n        node_type = node.get('type', 'unknown')\n        stats[\"node_types\"][node_type] = stats[\"node_types\"].get(node_type, 0) + 1\n\n    # Count edges per node\n    out_degree = {node['id']: 0 for node in nodes}\n    in_degree = {node['id']: 0 for node in nodes}\n\n    for edge in edges:\n        out_degree[edge['from']] += 1\n        in_degree[edge['to']] += 1\n\n    # Find isolated nodes (no incoming or outgoing edges except start)\n    first_node_id = nodes[0]['id'] if nodes else None\n    for node in nodes:\n        node_id = node['id']\n        if node_id != first_node_id and in_degree.get(node_id, 0) == 0:\n            stats[\"isolated_nodes\"].append(node_id)\n\n    if edges:\n        stats[\"avg_edges_per_node\"] = len(edges) / len(nodes)\n\n    return stats\n```\n\n## Comprehensive Validation\n\n```python\ndef validate_graph(nodes: list, edges: list) -> tuple[bool, dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Run all validations and return results.\n    Returns: (is_valid, validation_report)\n    \"\"\"\n    report = {\n        \"reachable\": False,\n        \"edges_valid\": False,\n        \"no_cycles\": False,\n        \"errors\": []\n    }\n\n    # Check reachability\n    reachable, unreachable = validate_reachability(nodes, edges)\n    report[\"reachable\"] = reachable\n    if not reachable:\n        report[\"errors\"].append(f\"Unreachable nodes: {unreachable}\")\n\n    # Check edges\n    edges_valid, missing = validate_edge_targets(nodes, edges)\n    report[\"edges_valid\"] = edges_valid\n    if not edges_valid:\n        report[\"errors\"].append(f\"Missing nodes: {missing}\")\n\n    # Check cycles\n    has_cycles, cycle = has_cycle(edges)\n    report[\"no_cycles\"] = not has_cycles\n    if has_cycles:\n        report[\"errors\"].append(f\"Cycle detected: {cycle}\")\n\n    is_valid = reachable and edges_valid and not has_cycles\n    return is_valid, report\n```\n\n## Testing Validation Functions\n\n```python\n# Example test\ntest_nodes = [\n    {\"id\": \"0\", \"text\": \"Start\", \"speaker\": \"\", \"type\": \"line\"},\n    {\"id\": \"1\", \"text\": \"Choice\", \"speaker\": \"\", \"type\": \"choice\"},\n    {\"id\": \"2\", \"text\": \"End\", \"speaker\": \"\", \"type\": \"line\"}\n]\n\ntest_edges = [\n    {\"from\": \"0\", \"to\": \"1\", \"text\": \"\"},\n    {\"from\": \"1\", \"to\": \"2\", \"text\": \"\"}\n]\n\nis_valid, report = validate_graph(test_nodes, test_edges)\nassert is_valid, f\"Validation failed: {report['errors']}\"\n```\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_vrtx_014wtwvL3DVqcvoHvrSs8iJm","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01En5EvWzbUVSQSZUAeEqK5F","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/solution.py","content":"import re\nimport json\nfrom typing import Dict, List, Set, Tuple\n\n\ndef parse_script(text: str) -> Dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Parse a dialogue script into a structured graph.\n\n    Returns a dictionary with:\n    - nodes: list of {\"id\", \"text\", \"speaker\", \"type\"}\n    - edges: list of {\"from\", \"to\", \"text\"}\n    \"\"\"\n\n    # Step 1: Parse all scenes and their content\n    scenes = parse_scenes(text)\n\n    # Step 2: Parse lines within each scene\n    parsed_scenes = {}\n    for scene_name in scenes:\n        content = get_scene_content(text, scene_name, scenes)\n        parsed_scenes[scene_name] = parse_scene_lines(content, scene_name)\n\n    # Step 3: Build nodes and edges\n    nodes = []\n    edges = []\n    node_id_counter = 0\n    scene_node_map = {}  # Map from scene_name to list of node_ids\n\n    for scene_name, lines in parsed_scenes.items():\n        scene_node_map[scene_name] = []\n\n        for line in lines:\n            node = {\n                \"id\": str(node_id_counter),\n                \"text\": line['text'],\n                \"speaker\": line.get('speaker', ''),\n                \"type\": line['type']\n            }\n            nodes.append(node)\n            scene_node_map[scene_name].append(node_id_counter)\n            line['node_id'] = str(node_id_counter)\n            node_id_counter += 1\n\n    # Step 4: Build edges from transitions\n    for scene_name, lines in parsed_scenes.items():\n        for line in lines:\n            if 'target' in line and line['target']:\n                source_id = line['node_id']\n                target_scene = line['target']\n\n                # Find the first node ID of the target scene\n                if target_scene in scene_node_map and scene_node_map[target_scene]:\n                    target_id = str(scene_node_map[target_scene][0])\n                    edge = {\n                        \"from\": source_id,\n                        \"to\": target_id,\n                        \"text\": \"\"\n                    }\n                    edges.append(edge)\n\n    return {\n        \"nodes\": nodes,\n        \"edges\": edges\n    }\n\n\ndef parse_scenes(text: str) -> Dict[str, int]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract all scene headers and their positions.\n    Returns dict mapping scene_name -> start_position\n    \"\"\"\n    scene_pattern = r'\\[([^\\]]+)\\]'\n    scenes = {}\n\n    for match in re.finditer(scene_pattern, text):\n        scene_name = match.group(1)\n        start_pos = match.end()\n        scenes[scene_name] = start_pos\n\n    return scenes\n\n\ndef get_scene_content(text: str, scene_name: str, all_scenes: Dict[str, int]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Extract content of a scene from the text.\n    \"\"\"\n    if scene_name not in all_scenes:\n        return \"\"\n\n    start = all_scenes[scene_name]\n\n    # Find the next scene's start position\n    scene_list = list(all_scenes.keys())\n    current_idx = scene_list.index(scene_name)\n\n    if current_idx < len(scene_list) - 1:\n        next_scene_name = scene_list[current_idx + 1]\n        next_start = all_scenes[next_scene_name]\n        # Go back to find the '[' of the next scene\n        end = text.rfind('[', 0, next_start)\n        if end == -1:\n            end = next_start\n    else:\n        end = len(text)\n\n    return text[start:end].strip()\n\n\ndef parse_scene_lines(content: str, scene_name: str) -> List[Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Parse individual lines within a scene.\n    Returns list of line dictionaries with type, text, speaker, and target.\n    \"\"\"\n    lines = []\n\n    # Pattern for speaker lines: \"Speaker: text -> Target\"\n    speaker_pattern = r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    # Pattern for choice lines: \"1. [Tag] text -> Target\" or \"1. text -> Target\"\n    choice_pattern = r'^\\d+\\.\\s*(\\[.+?\\]\\s*)?(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    current_choice_block = []\n    current_choice_id = None\n\n    for raw_line in content.split('\\n'):\n        line = raw_line.strip()\n\n        if not line:\n            # Empty line might signal end of choice block\n            if current_choice_block:\n                # We'll check on next non-empty line\n                pass\n            continue\n\n        # Try to match speaker line\n        speaker_match = re.match(speaker_pattern, line)\n        if speaker_match:\n            speaker, text, target = speaker_match.groups()\n\n            # Flush any pending choice block\n            if current_choice_block:\n                lines.append({\n                    'type': 'choice',\n                    'text': '\\n'.join(current_choice_block),\n                    'speaker': '',\n                    'target': current_choice_id\n                })\n                current_choice_block = []\n                current_choice_id = None\n\n            lines.append({\n                'type': 'line',\n                'speaker': speaker.strip(),\n                'text': text.strip(),\n                'target': target.strip()\n            })\n            continue\n\n        # Pattern for speaker lines: \"Speaker: text -> Target\"\n    speaker_pattern = r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    # Pattern for choice lines: \"1. [Tag] text -> Target\" or \"1. text -> Target\"\n    choice_pattern = r'^\\d+\\.\\s*(\\[.+?\\]\\s*)?(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    current_choices = []\n\n    for raw_line in content.split('\\n'):\n        line = raw_line.strip()\n\n        if not line:\n            # Empty line might signal end of choice block\n            if current_choices:\n                # We'll check on next non-empty line\n                pass\n            continue\n\n        # Try to match speaker line\n        speaker_match = re.match(speaker_pattern, line)\n        if speaker_match:\n            speaker, text, target = speaker_match.groups()\n\n            # Flush any pending choice block\n            if current_choices:\n                lines.append({\n                    'type': 'choice',\n                    'text': '\\n'.join([c['text'] for c in current_choices]),\n                    'speaker': '',\n                    'choice_targets': current_choices  # Store all choice targets\n                })\n                current_choices = []\n\n            lines.append({\n                'type': 'line',\n                'speaker': speaker.strip(),\n                'text': text.strip(),\n                'target': target.strip()\n            })\n            continue\n\n        # Pattern for speaker lines: \"Speaker: text -> Target\"\n    speaker_pattern = r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    # Pattern for choice lines: \"1. [Tag] text -> Target\" or \"1. text -> Target\"\n    choice_pattern = r'^\\d+\\.\\s*(\\[.+?\\]\\s*)?(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    current_choice_block = []\n    current_choice_id = None\n\n    for raw_line in content.split('\\n'):\n        line = raw_line.strip()\n\n        if not line:\n            # Start new choice block\n                current_choice_id = target.strip()\n                current_choice_block = [choice_text]\n            elif current_choice_id == target.strip():\n                # Escape special characters for DOT\n    text = escape_dot(text)\n\n    # Build label\n    if node_type == 'line' and speaker:\n        speaker = escape_dot(speaker)\n        label = f\"{speaker}:\\\\n{text}\"\n    else:\n        label = text\n\n    return label\n\n\ndef escape_dot(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Escape special characters for DOT format.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Escape backslash first\n    text = text.replace(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\")\n    # Escape quotes\n    text = text.replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n    # Convert newlines to \\n for display\n    text = text.replace(\"\\n\", \"\\\\n\")\n    return text\n\n\ndef write_files(graph: Dict, json_path: str, dot_path: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Write both JSON and DOT files.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        # Write JSON\n        with open(json_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(graph, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        # Generate and write DOT\n        dot_content = generate_dot(graph['nodes'], graph['edges'])\n        with open(dot_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            f.write(dot_content)\n\n        return True\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error writing files: {e}\")\n        return False\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    # Pattern for speaker lines: \"Speaker: text -> Target\"\n    speaker_pattern = r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    # Pattern for choice lines: \"1. Try to match choice line\n        choice_match = re.match(choice_pattern, line)\n        if choice_match:\n            tag, text, target = choice_match.groups()\n\n            choice_text = text.strip()\n            if tag:\n                choice_text = tag.strip() + ' ' + choice_text\n\n            current_choices.append({\n                'text': choice_text,\n                'target': target.strip()\n            })\n\n    # BFS from first node\n    visited = set()\n    queue = [first_node_id]\n    while queue:\n        node_id = queue.pop(0)\n        if node_id in visited:\n            continue\n        visited.add(node_id)\n        queue.extend(adj.get(node_id, []))\n\n    unreachable = node_ids - visited\n    report[\"reachable\"] = len(unreachable) == 0\n    if unreachable:\n        report[\"errors\"].append(f\"Unreachable nodes: {unreachable}\")\n        report[\"no_unreachable_nodes\"] = False\n\n    # Write JSON\n        with open(json_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(graph, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        # Generate and write DOT\n        dot_content = generate_dot(graph['nodes'], graph['edges'])\n        with open(dot_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            f.write(dot_content)\n\n        return True\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error writing files: {e}\")\n        return False\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    # Try to match choice line\n        choice_match = re.match(choice_pattern, line)\n        if choice_match:\n            tag, text, target = choice_match.groups()\n\n            choice_text = text.strip()\n            if tag:\n                choice_text = tag.strip() + ' ' + choice_text\n\n            result['choices'].append({\n                'text': choice_text,\n                'target': target.strip()\n            })\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef validate_graph(nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate the graph structure.\n    Returns: (is_valid, validation_report)\n    \"\"\"\n    report = {\n        \"reachable\": False,\n        \"edges_valid\": False,\n        \"no_unreachable_nodes\": True,\n        \"errors\": []\n    }\n\n    if not nodes:\n        return True, report\n\n    # Check 1: All nodes reachable from first\n    first_node_id = nodes[0]['id']\n    node_ids = {node['id'] for node in nodes}\n\n    # Build adjacency list\n    adj = {node_id: [] for node_id in node_ids}\n    for edge in edges:\n        if edge['from'] in adj:\n            adj[edge['from']].append(edge['to'])\n\n    # BFS from first node\n    visited = set()\n    queue = [first_node_id]\n    while queue:\n        node_id = queue.pop(0)\n        if node_id in visited:\n            continue\n        visited.add(node_id)\n        queue.extend(adj.get(node_id, []))\n\n    unreachable = node_ids - visited\n    report[\"reachable\"] = len(unreachable) == 0\n    if unreachable:\n        report[\"errors\"].append(f\"Unreachable nodes: {unreachable}\")\n        report[\"no_unreachable_nodes\"] = False\n\n    # Check 2: All edge targets exist\n    edges_valid = True\n    for edge in edges:\n        if edge['to'] not in node_ids:\n            report[\"errors\"].append(f\"Edge target doesn't exist: {edge['to']}\")\n            edges_valid = False\n\n    report[\"edges_valid\"] = edges_valid\n\n    return report[\"reachable\"] and edges_valid, report\n\n\ndef generate_dot(nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate DOT format string for Graphviz visualization.\n    \"\"\"\n    lines = []\n    lines.append(\"digraph dialogue {\")\n    lines.append(\"    rankdir=LR;\")\n    lines.append(\"    node [shape=box, style=rounded];\")\n\n    # Add nodes with styling\n    for node in nodes:\n        node_id = f\"node_{node['id']}\"\n        label = format_label(node)\n\n        if node['type'] == 'line':\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightblue, style=filled'\n        elif node['type'] == 'choice':\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightgreen, style=filled'\n        else:\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightyellow, style=filled'\n\n        lines.append(f\"    {node_id} [label=\\\"{label}\\\", {style}];\")\n\n    # Add edges\n    for edge in edges:\n        from_id = f\"node_{edge['from']}\"\n        to_id = f\"node_{edge['to']}\"\n        edge_text = edge.get('text', '').strip()\n\n        if edge_text:\n            lines.append(f\"    {from_id} -> {to_id} [label=\\\"{escape_dot(edge_text)}\\\"];\")\n        else:\n            lines.append(f\"    {from_id} -> {to_id};\")\n\n    lines.append(\"}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\ndef format_label(node: Dict) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Format node text for DOT display, escaping special characters.\n    \"\"\"\n    text = node['text']\n    speaker = node['speaker']\n    node_type = node['type']\n\n    # Escape special characters for DOT\n    text = escape_dot(text)\n\n    # Build label - already includes speaker if part of text\n    return text\n\n\ndef escape_dot(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Escape special characters for DOT format.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Escape backslash first\n    text = text.replace(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\")\n    # Escape quotes\n    text = text.replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n    # Convert newlines to \\n for display\n    text = text.replace(\"\\n\", \"\\\\n\")\n    return text\n\n\ndef write_files(graph: Dict, json_path: str, dot_path: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Write both JSON and DOT files.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        # Write JSON\n        with open(json_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(graph, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        # Generate and write DOT\n        dot_content = generate_dot(graph['nodes'], graph['edges'])\n        with open(dot_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            f.write(dot_content)\n\n        return True\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error writing files: {e}\")\n        return False\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    # Pattern for speaker lines: \"Speaker: text -> Target\"\n    speaker_pattern = r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    # Pattern for choice lines: \"1. [Tag] text -> Target\"\n    choice_pattern = r'^\\d+\\.\\s*(\\[.+?\\]\\s*)?(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    for raw_line in content.split('\\n'):\n        line = raw_line.strip()\n\n        if not line:\n            continue\n\n        # Try to match speaker line\n        speaker_match = re.match(speaker_pattern, line)\n        if speaker_match:\n            speaker, text, target = speaker_match.groups()\n            result['lines'].append({\n                'speaker': speaker.strip(),\n                'text': text.strip(),\n                'target': target.strip()\n            })\n            continue\n\n        # Try to match choice line\n        choice_match = re.match(choice_pattern, line)\n        if choice_match:\n            tag, text, target = choice_match.groups()\n\n            choice_text = text.strip()\n            if tag:\n                choice_text = tag.strip() + ' ' + choice_text\n\n            result['choices'].append({\n                'text': choice_text,\n                'target': target.strip()\n            })\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef validate_graph(nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate the graph structure.\n    Returns: (is_valid, validation_report)\n    \"\"\"\n    report = {\n        \"reachable\": False,\n        \"edges_valid\": False,\n        \"no_unreachable_nodes\": True,\n        \"errors\": []\n    }\n\n    if not nodes:\n        return True, report\n\n    # Check 1: All nodes reachable from first\n    first_node_id = nodes[0]['id']\n    node_ids = {node['id'] for node in nodes}\n\n    # Build adjacency list\n    adj = {node_id: [] for node_id in node_ids}\n    for edge in edges:\n        if edge['from'] in adj:\n            adj[edge['from']].append(edge['to'])\n\n    # BFS from first node\n    visited = set()\n    queue = [first_node_id]\n    while queue:\n        node_id = queue.pop(0)\n        if node_id in visited:\n            continue\n        visited.add(node_id)\n        queue.extend(adj.get(node_id, []))\n\n    unreachable = node_ids - visited\n    report[\"reachable\"] = len(unreachable) == 0\n    if unreachable:\n        report[\"errors\"].append(f\"Unreachable nodes: {unreachable}\")\n        report[\"no_unreachable_nodes\"] = False\n\n    # Check 2: All edge targets exist\n    edges_valid = True\n    for edge in edges:\n        if edge['to'] not in node_ids:\n            report[\"errors\"].append(f\"Edge target doesn't exist: {edge['to']}\")\n            edges_valid = False\n\n    report[\"edges_valid\"] = edges_valid\n\n    return report[\"reachable\"] and edges_valid, report\n\n\ndef generate_dot(nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate DOT format string for Graphviz visualization.\n    \"\"\"\n    lines = []\n    lines.append(\"digraph dialogue {\")\n    lines.append(\"    rankdir=LR;\")\n    lines.append(\"    node [shape=box, style=rounded];\")\n\n    # Add nodes with styling\n    for node in nodes:\n        node_id = f\"node_{node['id']}\"\n        label = format_label(node)\n\n        if node['type'] == 'line':\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightblue, style=filled'\n        elif node['type'] == 'choice':\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightgreen, style=filled'\n        else:\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightyellow, style=filled'\n\n        lines.append(f\"    {node_id} [label=\\\"{label}\\\", {style}];\")\n\n    # Add edges\n    for edge in edges:\n        from_id = f\"node_{edge['from']}\"\n        to_id = f\"node_{edge['to']}\"\n        edge_text = edge.get('text', '').strip()\n\n        if edge_text:\n            lines.append(f\"    {from_id} -> {to_id} [label=\\\"{escape_dot(edge_text)}\\\"];\")\n        else:\n            lines.append(f\"    {from_id} -> {to_id};\")\n\n    lines.append(\"}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\ndef format_label(node: Dict) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Format node text for DOT display, escaping special characters.\n    \"\"\"\n    text = node['text']\n\n    # Escape special characters for DOT\n    text = escape_dot(text)\n\n    # Build label - already includes speaker if part of text\n    return text\n\n\ndef escape_dot(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Escape special characters for DOT format.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Escape backslash first\n    text = text.replace(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\")\n    # Escape quotes\n    text = text.replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n    # Convert newlines to \\n for display\n    text = text.replace(\"\\n\", \"\\\\n\")\n    return text\n\n\ndef write_files(graph: Dict, json_path: str, dot_path: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Write both JSON and DOT files.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        # Write JSON\n        with open(json_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(graph, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        # Generate and write DOT\n        dot_content = generate_dot(graph['nodes'], graph['edges'])\n        with open(dot_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            f.write(dot_content)\n\n        return True\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error writing files: {e}\")\n        return False\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    # Step 3: Build nodes and edges\n    nodes = []\n    edges = []\n    scene_to_node_id = {}  # Map scene_name -> node_id\n\n    node_id = 0\n\n    # Create a node for each scene\n    for scene_name, scene_data in parsed_scenes.items():\n        # Combine all text content from the scene\n        text_parts = []\n\n        # Add speaker lines\n        for line in scene_data['lines']:\n            if line['speaker']:\n                text_parts.append(f\"{line['speaker']}: {line['text']}\")\n            else:\n                text_parts.append(line['text'])\n\n        combined_text = '\\n'.join(text_parts)\n\n        # Determine node type based on whether there are choices\n        if scene_data['choices']:\n            node_type = 'choice'\n            # Add choices to text\n            choice_texts = []\n            for i, choice in enumerate(scene_data['choices'], 1):\n                choice_texts.append(f\"{i}. {choice['text']}\")\n            combined_text += '\\n' + '\\n'.join(choice_texts)\n        else:\n            node_type = 'line'\n\n        # Find the next scene's start position\n    scene_list = list(all_scenes.keys())\n    current_idx = scene_list.index(scene_name)\n\n    if current_idx < len(scene_list) - 1:\n        next_scene_name = scene_list[current_idx + 1]\n        next_start = all_scenes[next_scene_name]\n        # Pattern for speaker lines: \"Speaker: text -> Target\"\n    speaker_pattern = r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    # Pattern for choice lines: \"1. [Tag] text -> Target\"\n    choice_pattern = r'^\\d+\\.\\s*(\\[.+?\\]\\s*)?(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    for raw_line in content.split('\\n'):\n        line = raw_line.strip()\n\n        if not line:\n            continue\n\n        # Try to match speaker line\n        speaker_match = re.match(speaker_pattern, line)\n        if speaker_match:\n            speaker, text, target = speaker_match.groups()\n            result['lines'].append({\n                'speaker': speaker.strip(),\n                'text': text.strip(),\n                'target': target.strip()\n            })\n            continue\n\n        # Try to match choice line\n        choice_match = re.match(choice_pattern, line)\n        if choice_match:\n            tag, text, target = choice_match.groups()\n\n            choice_text = text.strip()\n            if tag:\n                choice_text = tag.strip() + ' ' + choice_text\n\n            result['choices'].append({\n                'text': choice_text,\n                'target': target.strip()\n            })\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef validate_graph(nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate the graph structure.\n    Returns: (is_valid, validation_report)\n    \"\"\"\n    report = {\n        \"reachable\": False,\n        \"edges_valid\": False,\n        \"no_unreachable_nodes\": True,\n        \"errors\": []\n    }\n\n    if not nodes:\n        return True, report\n\n    # Check 1: All nodes reachable from first\n    first_node_id = nodes[0]['id']\n    node_ids = {node['id'] for node in nodes}\n\n    # Build adjacency list\n    adj = {node_id: [] for node_id in node_ids}\n    for edge in edges:\n        if edge['from'] in adj:\n            adj[edge['from']].append(edge['to'])\n\n    # BFS from first node\n    visited = set()\n    queue = [first_node_id]\n    while queue:\n        node_id = queue.pop(0)\n        if node_id in visited:\n            continue\n        visited.add(node_id)\n        queue.extend(adj.get(node_id, []))\n\n    unreachable = node_ids - visited\n    report[\"reachable\"] = len(unreachable) == 0\n    if unreachable:\n        report[\"errors\"].append(f\"Unreachable nodes: {unreachable}\")\n        report[\"no_unreachable_nodes\"] = False\n\n    # Check 2: All edge targets exist\n    edges_valid = True\n    for edge in edges:\n        if edge['to'] not in node_ids:\n            report[\"errors\"].append(f\"Edge target doesn't exist: {edge['to']}\")\n            edges_valid = False\n\n    report[\"edges_valid\"] = edges_valid\n\n    return report[\"reachable\"] and edges_valid, report\n\n\ndef generate_dot(nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate DOT format string for Graphviz visualization.\n    \"\"\"\n    lines = []\n    lines.append(\"digraph dialogue {\")\n    lines.append(\"    rankdir=LR;\")\n    lines.append(\"    node [shape=box, style=rounded];\")\n\n    # Add nodes with styling\n    for node in nodes:\n        node_id = f\"node_{node['id']}\"\n        label = format_label(node)\n\n        if node['type'] == 'line':\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightblue, style=filled'\n        elif node['type'] == 'choice':\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightgreen, style=filled'\n        else:\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightyellow, style=filled'\n\n        lines.append(f\"    {node_id} [label=\\\"{label}\\\", {style}];\")\n\n    # Add edges\n    for edge in edges:\n        from_id = f\"node_{edge['from']}\"\n        to_id = f\"node_{edge['to']}\"\n        edge_text = edge.get('text', '').strip()\n\n        if edge_text:\n            lines.append(f\"    {from_id} -> {to_id} [label=\\\"{escape_dot(edge_text)}\\\"];\")\n        else:\n            lines.append(f\"    {from_id} -> {to_id};\")\n\n    lines.append(\"}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\ndef format_label(node: Dict) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Format node text for DOT display, escaping special characters.\n    \"\"\"\n    text = node['text']\n\n    # Escape special characters for DOT\n    text = escape_dot(text)\n\n    # Build label - already includes speaker if part of text\n    return text\n\n\ndef escape_dot(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Escape special characters for DOT format.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Escape backslash first\n    text = text.replace(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\")\n    # Escape quotes\n    text = text.replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n    # Convert newlines to \\n for display\n    text = text.replace(\"\\n\", \"\\\\n\")\n    return text\n\n\ndef write_files(graph: Dict, json_path: str, dot_path: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Write both JSON and DOT files.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        # Pattern for speaker lines: \"Speaker: text -> Target\"\n    speaker_pattern = r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    # Pattern for choice lines: \"1. [Tag] text -> Target\" or \"1. text -> Target\"\n    choice_pattern = r'^\\d+\\.\\s*(\\[.+?\\]\\s*)?(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    current_choices = []\n\n    for raw_line in content.split('\\n'):\n        line = raw_line.strip()\n\n        if not line:\n            # Empty line might signal end of choice block\n            if current_choices:\n                # We'll check on next non-empty line\n                pass\n            continue\n\n        # Try to match speaker line\n        speaker_match = re.match(speaker_pattern, line)\n        if speaker_match:\n            speaker, text, target = speaker_match.groups()\n\n            # Flush any pending choice block\n            if current_choices:\n                lines.append({\n                    'type': 'choice',\n                    'text': '\\n'.join([c['text'] for c in current_choices]),\n                    'speaker': '',\n                    'choice_targets': current_choices  # Store all choice targets\n                })\n                current_choices = []\n\n            lines.append({\n                'type': 'line',\n                'speaker': speaker.strip(),\n                'text': text.strip(),\n                'target': target.strip()\n            })\n            continue\n\n        # Try to match choice line\n        choice_match = re.match(choice_pattern, line)\n        if choice_match:\n            tag, text, target = choice_match.groups()\n\n            choice_text = text.strip()\n            if tag:\n                choice_text = tag.strip() + ' ' + choice_text\n\n            current_choices.append({\n                'text': choice_text,\n                'target': target.strip()\n            })\n\n    # Flush any remaining choice block\n    if current_choices:\n        lines.append({\n            'type': 'choice',\n            'text': '\\n'.join([c['text'] for c in current_choices]),\n            'speaker': '',\n            'choice_targets': current_choices\n        })\n\n    return lines\n\n\ndef validate_graph(nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate the graph structure.\n    Returns: (is_valid, validation_report)\n    \"\"\"\n    report = {\n        \"reachable\": False,\n        \"edges_valid\": False,\n        \"no_unreachable_nodes\": True,\n        \"errors\": []\n    }\n\n    if not nodes:\n        return True, report\n\n    # Check 1: All nodes reachable from first\n    first_node_id = nodes[0]['id']\n    node_ids = {node['id'] for node in nodes}\n\n    # Build adjacency list\n    adj = {node_id: [] for node_id in node_ids}\n    for edge in edges:\n        if edge['from'] in adj:\n            adj[edge['from']].append(edge['to'])\n\n    # BFS from first node\n    visited = set()\n    queue = [first_node_id]\n    while queue:\n        node_id = queue.pop(0)\n        if node_id in visited:\n            continue\n        visited.add(node_id)\n        queue.extend(adj.get(node_id, []))\n\n    unreachable = node_ids - visited\n    report[\"reachable\"] = len(unreachable) == 0\n    if unreachable:\n        report[\"errors\"].append(f\"Unreachable nodes: {unreachable}\")\n        report[\"no_unreachable_nodes\"] = False\n\n    # Check 2: All edge targets exist\n    edges_valid = True\n    for edge in edges:\n        if edge['to'] not in node_ids:\n            report[\"errors\"].append(f\"Edge target doesn't exist: {edge['to']}\")\n            edges_valid = False\n\n    report[\"edges_valid\"] = edges_valid\n\n    return report[\"reachable\"] and edges_valid, report\n\n\ndef generate_dot(nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Generate DOT format string for Graphviz visualization.\n    \"\"\"\n    lines = []\n    lines.append(\"digraph dialogue {\")\n    lines.append(\"    rankdir=LR;\")\n    lines.append(\"    node [shape=box, style=rounded];\")\n\n    # Add nodes with styling\n    for node in nodes:\n        node_id = f\"node_{node['id']}\"\n        label = format_label(node)\n\n        if node['type'] == 'line':\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightblue, style=filled'\n        elif node['type'] == 'choice':\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightgreen, style=filled'\n        else:\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightyellow, style=filled'\n\n        lines.append(f\"    {node_id} [label=\\\"{label}\\\", {style}];\")\n\n    # Add edges\n    for edge in edges:\n        from_id = f\"node_{edge['from']}\"\n        to_id = f\"node_{edge['to']}\"\n        edge_text = edge.get('text', '').strip()\n\n        if edge_text:\n            lines.append(f\"    {from_id} -> {to_id} [label=\\\"{escape_dot(edge_text)}\\\"];\")\n        else:\n            lines.append(f\"    {from_id} -> {to_id};\")\n\n    lines.append(\"}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\ndef format_label(node: Dict) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Format node text for DOT display, escaping special characters.\n    \"\"\"\n    text = node['text']\n    speaker = node['speaker']\n    node_type = node['type']\n\n    # Escape special characters for DOT\n    text = escape_dot(text)\n\n    # Build label\n    if node_type == 'line' and speaker:\n        speaker = escape_dot(speaker)\n        label = f\"{speaker}:\\\\n{text}\"\n    else:\n        label = text\n\n    return label\n\n\ndef escape_dot(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Escape special characters for DOT format.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Escape backslash first\n    text = text.replace(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\")\n    # Escape quotes\n    text = text.replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n    # Convert newlines to \\n for display\n    text = text.replace(\"\\n\", \"\\\\n\")\n    return text\n\n\ndef write_files(graph: Dict, json_path: str, dot_path: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Write both JSON and DOT files.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        # Write JSON\n        with open(json_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            json.dump(graph, f, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False)\n\n        # Generate and write DOT\n        dot_content = generate_dot(graph['nodes'], graph['edges'])\n        with open(dot_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n            f.write(dot_content)\n\n        return True\n    except Exception as e:\n        print(f\"Error writing files: {e}\")\n        return False\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    # Read input script\n    with open('/app/script.txt', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:\n        script_text = f.read()\n\n    # Parse the script\n    graph = parse_script(script_text)\n\n    # Validate\n    is_valid, report = validate_graph(graph['nodes'], graph['edges'])\n\n    if not is_valid:\n        print(\"Validation errors:\")\n        for error in report['errors']:\n            print(f\"  - {error}\")\n    else:\n        print(\"Validation passed!\")\n        print(f\"  - Nodes: {len(graph['nodes'])}\")\n        print(f\"  - Edges: {len(graph['edges'])}\")\n\n    # Write output files\n    write_files(graph, '/app/dialogue.json', '/app/dialogue.dot')\n    print(\"\\nOutput files written:\")\n    print(\"  - /app/dialogue.json\")\n    print(\"  - /app/dialogue.dot\")\n"}}

Determine node type based on whether there are choices\n        if scene_data['choices']:\n            node_type = 'choice'\n            # Add choices to text\n            choice_texts = []\n            for i, choice in enumerate(scene_data['choices'], 1):\n                choice_texts.append(f\"{i}. {choice['text']}\")\n            combined_text += '\\n' + '\\n'.join(choice_texts)\n        else:\n            node_type = 'line'\n\n        # Pattern for speaker lines: \"Speaker: text -> Target\"\n    speaker_pattern = r'^([^:]+):\\s*(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    # Pattern for choice lines: \"1. [Tag] text -> Target\"\n    choice_pattern = r'^\\d+\\.\\s*(\\[.+?\\]\\s*)?(.+?)\\s*->\\s*(\\w+)$'\n\n    for raw_line in content.split('\\n'):\n        line = raw_line.strip()\n\n        if not line:\n            continue\n\n        # Try to match speaker line\n        speaker_match = re.match(speaker_pattern, line)\n        if speaker_match:\n            speaker, text, target = speaker_match.groups()\n            result['lines'].append({\n                'speaker': speaker.strip(),\n                'text': text.strip(),\n                'target': target.strip()\n            })\n            continue\n\n        # Try to match choice line\n        choice_match = re.match(choice_pattern, line)\n        if choice_match:\n            tag, text, target = choice_match.groups()\n\n            choice_text = text.strip()\n            if tag:\n                choice_text = tag.strip() + ' ' + choice_text\n\n            result['choices'].append({\n                'text': choice_text,\n                'target': target.strip()\n            })\n\n    return result\n\n\ndef validate_graph(nodes: List[Dict], edges: List[Dict]) -> Tuple[bool, Dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Validate the graph structure.\n    Returns: (is_valid, validation_report)\n    \"\"\"\n    report = {\n        \"reachable\": False,\n        \"edges_valid\": False,\n        \"no_unreachable_nodes\": True,\n        \"errors\": []\n    }\n\n    if not nodes:\n        return True, report\n\n    # Add nodes with styling\n    for node in nodes:\n        node_id = f\"node_{node['id']}\"\n        label = format_label(node)\n\n        if node['type'] == 'line':\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightblue, style=filled'\n        elif node['type'] == 'choice':\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightgreen, style=filled'\n        else:\n            style = 'fillcolor=lightyellow, style=filled'\n\n        lines.append(f\"    {node_id} [label=\\\"{label}\\\", {style}];\")\n\n    # Add edges\n    for edge in edges:\n        from_id = f\"node_{edge['from']}\"\n        to_id = f\"node_{edge['to']}\"\n        edge_text = edge.get('text', '').strip()\n\n        if edge_text:\n            lines.append(f\"    {from_id} -> {to_id} [label=\\\"{escape_dot(edge_text)}\\\"];\")\n        else:\n            lines.append(f\"    {from_id} -> {to_id};\")\n\n    lines.append(\"}\")\n    return \"\\n\".join(lines)\n\n\ndef format_label(node: Dict) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Format node text for DOT display, escaping special characters.\n    \"\"\"\n    text = node['text']\n\n    # Escape special characters for DOT\n    text = escape_dot(text)\n\n    # Build label - already includes speaker if part of text\n    return text\n\n\ndef escape_dot(text: str) -> str:\n    \"\"\"\n    Escape special characters for DOT format.\n    \"\"\"\n    # Escape backslash first\n    text = text.replace(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\")\n    # Escape quotes\n    text = text.replace('\"', '\\\\\"')\n    # Convert newlines to \\n for display\n    text = text.replace(\"\\n\", \"\\\\n\")\n    return text\n\n\ndef write_files(graph: Dict, json_path: str, dot_path: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"\n    Write both JSON and DOT files.\n    \"\"\"\n    try:\n        #
